				To avoid single use plastic, KSRTC has discontinued distribution of water bottles  in premium services frm 3rd October 2019. Passengers are requested to co-operate and bring their own water bottles. "Say No to Single Use Plastic - Save Earth"
				| New Non AC sleeper services introduced from Bengaluru to Munnar w.e.f 25-04-2019.
				| New Ambaari Dream Class(Volvo Multiaxle AC Sleeper) services introduced from Bengaluru to Ernakulam, Pune, Secunderabad and Vijayawada.
				| Package Tour introduced from Mysore to Tirumala  &amp; from Mangaluru/Shivamogga/Davanagere to Tirumala-Srikalahasti w.e.f 20-7-18.
           		| Now KSTDC Package tour bookings are available in KSRTC website
           		| Newer version of KSRTC Android &amp; IOS APP available.
           		| Package Tour introduced from Bengaluru to Tirumala w.e.f. 12-05-2017.
           		| Advance booking is allowed for 30 days.
           		| Payment gateway charges withdrawn from 01-09-16.</marquee>
